- **scylladb-data-modeling**: ScyllaDB CQL data modeling patterns and anti-patterns, including compaction strategy choice, TTL-based expiration, tombstone management, and lightweight transactions. Use when designing tables, reviewing schemas, migrating from SQL or MongoDB, setting data retention, or troubleshooting performance issues caused by schema problems or deletes, or writing conditional (LWT) statements. Vector and full-text indexes go to scylladb-vector-search; DynamoDB API tables go to scylladb-alternator. - **sd-debug**: 当遇到 bug、测试失败、非预期行为、报错信息、接口异常、页面不显示、数据不对时使用。即使用户只说"这里不对""跑不起来""报错了""为什么结果不对""坏了""挂了""出问题了""不工作"也应触发。核心方法论是在提出修复方案前先完成系统化的根因调查，避免盲目猜测修复。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/debug) · ★1)
- **sd-debug**: The disciplined debug/fix loop the generic agent runs when a sd-verify / sd-test run FAILS (/foundry:sd-debug, software-delivery debug step, step 9). A PROCEDURE — reproduce → isolate → root-cause → minimal-fix → re-verify, ONE hypothesis per iteration (NOT shotgun edits), BOUNDED so it converges (the failure is fixed and re-verified) or escalates to the operator with a structured hand-off. Advisory: it disciplines a debugging process; it does NOT gate, approve, or merge. ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-debug))

([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-prose-lint) · ★1)

- **sd-raw-rescue**: Rescata fotos RAW (Sony ARW/SR2, Nikon NEF, Canon CR2, DNG) y JPG de tarjetas SD/CF dañadas, borradas, formateadas o con el directorio roto, siempre en SOLO LECTURA y sobre una imagen 1:1 de la tarjeta. Rearma lo que PhotoRec/TestDisk dejan roto (bandas magenta, RAW que se ve bien en miniatura y se rompe al abrirlo, nombres con basura en DCIM, archivos de 0 bytes) usando la FAT y los volcados de directorio que quedan en la tarjeta, saca fotos borradas por cabecera TIFF y repara filas faltantes de un ARW con el JPEG embebido. Usar SIEMPRE que el usuario quiera recuperar fotos o archivos de una… ([richardcaffaratti14-creator/sd-raw-rescue](https://github.com/richardcaffaratti14-creator/sd-raw-rescue/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **sd-recon-engine**: 【套装内部技能 · 全流程用户通常无需直接调用；直接说需求给总控 short-drama-director 即可，总控会自动路由到本技能】【漫剧老李 V7.5.0-SLIM 套装 · 子技能 · 〇·1 素材来源锁 · 对标采风】素材采风与对标情报引擎（轻量版）：用户说「我要对标某个爆款」时，把该剧的公开信息从全网（含外网）找回来，按固定格式交付一份《对标情报卡》。核心能力——短剧信源分级清单（平台官方榜 / 数据指数站 / 行业媒体 / 社媒讨论 / 外网指数）、采风四步（定目标→定信源→检索→落卡）、对标情报卡十三项字段模板（含可迁移点与不可迁移点）、六条证据纪律（不编造 / 时点必标 / 来源必标 / 一手与转述分开 / 事实与推断分开 / 独立信源数才是热度参考）。触发词：对标爆款、扒一下、采风、找资料、收集信息、热点、爆款分析、参考这部、题材调研、品类调研、市场调研。本技能为套装成员，完整全流程从总控 short-drama-director 进入；〇·1 素材来源锁由总控选定。 ([lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill](https://github.com/lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill/tree/HEAD/sd-recon-engine) · ★2.2k)

- **sd-regression**: Catch unintended visual changes in the SMART Disburse onboarding — from colleagues' commits (shared checkbox, scripts, styles) or from our own work. Lists which commits reach SD pages, then screenshots every SD page and dialog before/after at 375/768/1280 and shows only what changed. Use at the start of the day, before taking in origin/main, before pushing, or when asked "what did &lt;colleague&gt; change that affects SD". ([vasileschidu/test-sx](https://github.com/vasileschidu/test-sx/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-regression) · ★1)
- **sd-replan-loop**: Sanctioned mid-execution re-plan protocol for specwright. Lets a workflow adapt when execution (or the batch review) reveals that `02-tasks.md` is wrong, **without** violating spec immutability or skipping a gate. Referenced at runtime by `/sd:feature` and `/sd:refactor` - the only two workflows that produce a `01-plan.md` + `02-tasks.md` pair to re-plan. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-replan-loop) · ★10)
- **sd-repos**: Quick reference for SD/SIC (Sustainability Dashboard/Sustainability Insight Center) repositories. Use when answering questions about (1) which repositories are SD/SIC repos, (2) which repos are ADS apps, (3) where repositories are located locally or remotely, (4) repository categorization, (5) SD terminology equivalence, (6) management scripts in SD directory, or (7) deployment/Kubernetes information for SD applications. Reads ONLY the test_recipe phase — the static_validation phase belongs to the separate sd… ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-test))

It IGNORES the test_recipe records (the separate sd-test step). - **sd-workflow**: 当用户描述一个开发意图但不确定该从脑暴、写规格还是开发开始时使用。简单想法、信息不足、需要思考或脑暴时进入 brainstorm；已有较详细 PRD、技术文档、需求说明或明确规格素材时进入 write-spec；如果已经有 spec 文档并明确要推进实现，可以进入 dev。workflow 不直接进入 review/debug/parallel。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/workflow) · ★1)
- **sd-write-spec**: 当用户需求已基本明确、希望生成结构化的规格文档、或明确说"写规格""写 spec""整理需求"时使用。基于 EARS 方法生成需求规格说明。如果需求还很模糊，应先使用 sd-brainstorm 收敛。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/write-spec) · ★1)

- **sd2-pe**: Seedance 2.0 video prompt engineering base (八大要素, 全能参考绑定). Decide by what the user wants produced. - **sda-adr-list**: Lista ADRs por status e escopo usando o índice do projeto. Use para localizar decisões arquiteturais. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-list))
- **sda-adr-reindex**: Reconstrói e valida o índice de ADRs a partir dos arquivos existentes. Use após mudanças ou inconsistências. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-reindex))
- **sda-adr-review**: Revisa qualidade, validade e aderência de ADRs ao código atual. Use em auditorias arquiteturais. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-review))
- **sda-adr-show**: Exibe uma ADR e suas relações com código, specs e decisões sucessoras. Use para consultar uma decisão. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-show))
- **sda-adr-supersede**: Substitui uma ADR aceita por uma nova decisão e atualiza relações e índice. Use após mudança arquitetural explícita. ([LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent](https://github.com/LuisFelipeUnzueta/spec-driven-agent/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/sda-adr-supersede))
- **sda-backend-contract-handoff**: Gera handoff verificável de contratos backend para consumidores frontend. Use após estabilizar endpoints e schemas.
